STEP0_VERDICT: UNVERIFIED

Decision for Shakur: Keep Step 0 open. Both candidates pass measured correctness, timing and document-read gates, but neither reproduces every timing percentile within ±20%, and application-call instrumentation does not fulfill the engine-native range-evidence requirement.

Recommended design: slots. Slots avoid the values design's 12 extra rows per record and measured hydration cost, with 50 rather than 100 documents read per page. Production still needs a bounded allocation contract and validation for several typed slots per object, such as 8 number, 8 string, 4 date and 4 boolean, since this spike exercises only n0 and s0.

Done and independently checked

Verifier: Astra, fresh verification session. Repository commit 580dd54396cb5bda965e63f38724b93a96b5ad68, branch spike/records. Target: dev:gallant-pika-581. No tracked files edited, no commit, no deployment, no delegation and no reset.

  • pnpm typecheck and pnpm test exited 0; 3 test files and 13 tests passed.
  • pnpm seed exited 0 and reconciled exactly 50,000 records with 12 fields per candidate. Deterministic stage counts: new 20,000, contacted 15,000, qualified 7,500, proposal 5,000, won 2,500; scores 0 through 9,999.
  • pnpm bench exited 0; 520 samples in 26 groups. Every group has 20 samples. Each candidate has 240 baseline samples across three runs and 20 recovery samples.
  • All returned ID/order assertions passed, including cursor page 2 and the recovery top 50.
  • Independent Python cross-check matched all 520 measurements to uncached, successful engine completions and recomputed all 26 p50/p95 pairs from sorted raw durations.
  • Engine logs confirm successful recovery cleanup for both candidates: one document removed for slots, thirteen for values.

Oracle independence

bench/fixture.ts expected() uses ordinary in-memory filtering, sorting and slicing. It never imports or calls the Convex page query or uses its returned page/cursor to generate expected IDs. It shares fixture generation with seeding, while cli.ts reconciled() checks each live payload and source ID against that fixture. Captured system IDs and creation times supply identity and tie breaks. Correctness remains conditional on the stated descending creation-time/string-ID ordering convention.

Evidence judgment

RANGE_EVIDENCE: REJECTED as a substitute for engine-native evidence. The counter accurately instruments the visible database calls: one indexed pagination for slots; one pagination plus fifty primary-key gets for values. It does not observe engine index ranges, and this repository supplies no demonstrated mapping or upper-bound proof equating those calls with the engine range metric. Native document counts and successful queries do not establish the requested strict under-4,096 range assertion. The installed SDK completion-log type exposes executionTime and databaseReadDocuments but no native range field. This is an evidence gap, not a demonstrated range-limit violation.

WITHIN_20_PERCENT: no for both candidates against both builder runs. Compare absolute relative differences per matching group and percentile, using the builder value as denominator. Against cleanup, slots has 13/26 percentile comparisons outside tolerance and values 14/26. Against the earlier Convex run, slots has 12/26 outside and values 8/26. All 78 groups across all three reports remain under p50 100 ms and p95 250 ms, with identical document and instrumented-call counts per candidate. Classification: consistent with Cloud variance, no demonstrated gate-breaking regression; these observations do not prove the causal source of variation or satisfy the literal repeatability clause.

Risks and remaining gate conditions

  • Engine-native range evidence remains absent; require that evidence or an explicit approved amendment of the evidence requirement.
  • The literal ±20% repeatability criterion failed. Any variance allowance must be explicit rather than silently counted as a pass.
  • The slots prototype proves one numeric and one string slot, not the proposed production allocation or its indexing, write cost, exhaustion and migration behavior.
  • The values design implies 600,000 additional field rows for this 50,000-record fixture. Reconciliation checks record payloads, not the complete cardinality and integrity of all those rows.
  • Twenty samples per group give a coarse p95, the nineteenth sorted observation; these runs do not establish a production workload distribution.
  • No arbitrary compound-filter planner, concurrency or production write path was certified by this spike.
